@30.20

@30.21

@30.22

@30.23

@30.24

@30.25

@699.8
The one who controls a trade area is the "merchant chief".
@699.9
To win a trade area, you must at the very least have a branch in it.
@699.10

@699.16

@699.17
What kind of benefits...?
@699.18
One tenth of all trade on the routes in that trade area goes to the merchant chief as a fee.
@699.19
Conversely, trading in an area where you aren't merchant chief means paying that area's chief a one-tenth fee.
@699.20

@699.21
You can't become merchant chief just by declaring it. You need a certain standing in that trade area.
@699.22
For that, first become purveyor to the daimyo house that holds the trade area.
@699.23
Because the merchant chief of each trade area is appointed by its most powerful daimyo.
@699.24

@699.25

@699.28

@699.29

@699.30

@701.38

@701.39

@701.40

@701.41
